"Audio thread: real-time priority ({0})" = "Thread audio: prioritas real-time ({0})"
"Audio thread: normal priority, so the sound breaks up more easily under load. To allow real-time priority, install rtkit (most desktops have it) or add yourself to the \"audio\" group with an rtprio limit (/etc/security/limits.d), then log in again. LYNX SHARE never needs root." = "Thread audio: prioritas normal, sehingga suara lebih mudah putus saat beban tinggi. LYNX SHARE tidak pernah memerlukan root."

"Real-time priority" = "Prioritas real-time"
"The sound broke up, and Linux runs LYNX SHARE's audio thread at normal priority. Real-time priority makes dropouts much rarer:\n\n- install rtkit (package \"rtkit\"; most desktops already have it), or\n- add yourself to the \"audio\" group with an rtprio limit in /etc/security/limits.d (as for JACK),\n\nthen log out and in again. LYNX SHARE never needs root. Options > Audio settings shows the current state." = "Suara putus, dan Linux menjalankan thread audio LYNX SHARE dengan prioritas normal. # --- Round 163c: sample pitch (audio clips follow the pitch, keep length, Pitch...)
"The sample's pitch in semitones (0.5 = 50 cents). The Piano Roll's keys play it chromatically around its root note (Misc › Root note, C5 by default); right-click the channel › Pitch... for semitones + cents" = "Pitch sample dalam semitone (0,5 = 50 sen). Tuts Piano Roll memainkannya secara kromatik di sekitar nada dasarnya (Misc › Nada dasar, bawaan C5); klik kanan channel › Pitch... untuk semitone + sen"
"Keep length (stretch engine)" = "Jaga panjang (stretch)"
"On: the pitch, the fine tune and the keys change the pitch without changing the length, and \"Stretch\" changes the length without changing the pitch (like FL's Stretch modes). Off: resample, pitch and speed change together" = "Aktif: pitch, setel halus dan tuts mengubah pitch tanpa mengubah panjang, dan \"Stretch\" mengubah panjang tanpa mengubah pitch (seperti mode Stretch di FL). Nonaktif: resample, pitch dan kecepatan berubah bersama"
"The audio channel's pitch in semitones (0.5 = 50 cents): every clip of this file follows it. With \"Keep length\" the length stays, else the speed changes like a tape" = "Pitch channel audio dalam semitone (0,5 = 50 sen): setiap clip dari file ini mengikutinya. Dengan \"Jaga panjang\" panjangnya tetap, jika tidak kecepatannya berubah seperti tape"
"On: the stretch and the pitch change separately (the stretch keeps the pitch, the pitch keeps the length). Off: resample, pitch and speed change together" = "Aktif: stretch dan pitch berubah terpisah (stretch mempertahankan pitch, pitch mempertahankan panjang). Nonaktif: resample, pitch dan kecepatan berubah bersama"
"Pitch... (semitones, cents, keep length)" = "Pitch... (semitone, sen, pertahankan panjang)"

"Cents (-100 to +100)" = "Sen (-100 hingga +100)"
"Off: resample, like a tape: higher is shorter, lower is longer." = "Nonaktif: resample seperti tape: nada naik memendek, nada turun memanjang."
